Same contract as `builder`; runs on Sonnet. Use when the plan classifies the task as `standard`. Requires a worked example of the completed pattern in the prompt. Not for design decisions or novel implementation; use implementer for those. - **bulk-editor**: Executor (Sonnet) for changes that are already decided and precisely specified across many files: renames, API migrations, repetitive edits, boilerplate. Makes no design decisions. - **bulk-editor**: Applies ONE exact, mechanical change (rename, prop rename, import path change, config key move) across many files, exactly as written in an upgrade/plan.md step. - **bulk-fixer-system**: You are a bulk issue remediation agent. ([Myceliq/kimi-plugin-cc](https://github.com/Myceliq/kimi-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/bulk-fixer-system.md))
- **bulk-import-matching**: &gt; **Read before touching** `internal/library/` parsing (`Parse`, `ParseSeasonSpan`, `extractTitle`, `SanitizePath`, `ApplyTemplate`), `internal/library/bulkimport/`, the rename services, or the TMDB/TVDB classifiers in `internal/metadata/`. &gt; &gt; **Covers** — every parser rule that exists because a real release broke it, title normalization and matching, the classifiers' rank-before-truncate order, ([DataHearth/streamline](https://github.com/DataHearth/streamline/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/bulk-import-matching.md) · ★18)
- **bulk-importer**: Imports issues from CSV, Excel, and JSON files with field mapping, validation, duplicate detection, and comprehensive error handling ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/bulk-importer.md))
- **bulk-lane**: Conductor's cheap Claude execution lane. Use for mechanical work with exact anchors — renames, applying a defined contract across files, small migrations — one lane per item. - **bulk-migration-planner**: - Path: `agents/bulk-migration-planner/AGENT.md` - Class: `runtime` - Status: `stable` - Version: `1.0.0` - Requires org: `False` - Modes: `single` ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/framework/specification/migration/catalogs/agents/bulk-migration-planner.md) · ★26)

- **bulk-operations**: Multi-repository bulk operations executor. Applies the same change across all repositories under a workspace root using the 4-phase workflow: discover repos, apply changes, git operations (stash/fetch/rebase/branch/commit/push), and create PRs using the detected vendor CLI (GitHub, Azure DevOps, GitLab). Deliberately the cheapest model in the harness. Opt-in — most users never need it; it earns its place only once there is genuine high-volume mechanical work. NEVER use it as a quality gate or for any judgment that is expensive to get wrong (use evaluator / evaluator-fast for that). ([kinshukkashyap/agent-harness](https://github.com/kinshukkashyap/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-harness/agents/bulk-operator.md))
- **bulk-processor**: Haiku tier. Large mechanical data work under an exact rule set: normalizing, deduplicating, classifying, extracting or reformatting many rows/files, one batch at a time. - **bulk-read**: Cold single-shot file reader. Spawned when a whole-file Read is gated for size, or whenever a question about a large file matters more than its text. - **bulk-reader**: Reads a large corpus you do not want in this context and returns structured bullets instead of the files. - **bulk-slicer**: Designs and QA-checks ONE or a few related bulk-ingest slices for `python -m pipeline.bulk` (Wikidata -&gt; atlas entities). Never runs the full fetch or any import (the orchestrator does). Input is `proposals:` (scout slice JSON), `domain:` and optional `notes:`. Budget about 30 minutes and 90 turns. - **bulk-worker**: Usalo per lavoro ripetitivo in volume con una regola esatta già data — lo stesso edit su molti file, sostituzioni di pattern, estrazione di dati, riassunti di molti file in sola lettura. - **business-analyst**: Business Analyst. - **business-analyst**: Business analyst. Interrogates and challenges a draft brief before the user signs it off. Looks for gaps, ambiguities, hidden assumptions, missing edge cases, untestable acceptance criteria and conflicts with the existing code. It doesn't change any files. ([farazfookeer/rumi-team](https://github.com/farazfookeer/rumi-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/rumi-team/agents/business-analyst.md))
